 * Auxiliary Constructions:
: Points


 * Proof steps:

003. O is the circumcenter of \Delta CBA [08] & H is midpoint of BC [09] ⇒  ∠CAB = ∠COH [10]

005. ∠OCA = ∠ODA [11] ⇒  C,O,A,D are concyclic [12]

007. ∠ECO = ∠EAO [13] ⇒  E,C,O,A are concyclic [14]
008. C,O,A,D are concyclic [12] & E,C,O,A are concyclic [14] ⇒  A,O,E,D are concyclic

A B C D E F : Points
DB = DA [00]
∠CDE = ∠EDA [01]
A,E,C are collinear [02]
∠CDF = ∠FDB [03]
F,B,C are collinear [04]

 * Auxiliary Constructions:
: Points


 * Proof steps:
001. ∠ADE = ∠EDC [01] & A,E,C are collinear [02] ⇒  EC:AE = CD:AD [05]
002. ∠BDF = ∠FDC [03] & F,B,C are collinear [04] ⇒  FB:FC = DB:DC [06]
003. EC:AE = CD:AD [05] & FB:FC = DB:DC [06] & DB = DA [00] ⇒  CF:FB = CE:EA [07]
004. CF:FB = CE:EA [07] & F,B,C are collinear [04] & A,E,C are collinear [02] ⇒  FE ∥ BA

==========================
 * From theorem premises:
A B C D E F G : Points
CB = CA [00]
B,C,A are collinear [01]
CD = CA [02]
AE ⟂ AB [03]
DE ⟂ CD [04]
AB ⟂ FB [05]
D,E,F are collinear [06]
F,G,A are collinear [07]
E,B,G are collinear [08]

 * Auxiliary Constructions:
: Points


 * Proof steps:
001. AB ⟂ FB [05] & AE ⟂ AB [03] ⇒  FB ∥ AE [09]
002. BF ∥ AE [09] & F,G,A are collinear [07] & E,B,G are collinear [08] ⇒  FG:GA = BF:EA [10]
003. CD = CA [02] & CB = CA [00] ⇒  C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDA [11]
004. C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDA [11] & DE ⟂ CD [04] ⇒  ∠BDE = ∠BAD [12]
005. C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDA [11] & DE ⟂ CD [04] ⇒  ∠ADE = ∠ABD [13]
006. B,C,A are collinear [01] & AB ⟂ BF [05] ⇒  CB ⟂ BF [14]
007. C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDA [11] & CB ⟂ BF [14] ⇒  ∠FBD = ∠BAD [15]
008. D,E,F are collinear [06] & ∠BDE = ∠BAD [12] & ∠FBD = ∠BAD [15] & BF ∥ AE [09] ⇒  ∠FBD = ∠BDF [16]
009. ∠FBD = ∠BDF [16] ⇒  FB = FD [17]
010. B,C,A are collinear [01] & AB ⟂ AE [03] ⇒  CA ⟂ AE [18]
011. C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDA [11] & CA ⟂ AE [18] ⇒  ∠EAD = ∠ABD [19]
012. ∠EAD = ∠ABD [19] & ∠ADE = ∠ABD [13] ⇒  ∠ADE = ∠EAD [20]
013. ∠ADE = ∠EAD [20] ⇒  EA = ED [21]
014. BF:EA = FG:GA [10] & DF = BF [17] & DE = EA [21] ⇒  FD:DE = FG:GA [22]
015. FD:DE = FG:GA [22] & D,E,F are collinear [06] & F,G,A are collinear [07] ⇒  DG ∥ EA

==========================
 * From theorem premises:
B A C D E G F : Points
A,B,C are collinear [00]
AD = AB [01]
AE = AB [02]
CG = CB [03]
G,E,D are collinear [04]
CF = CB [05]
E,F,D are collinear [06]

 * Auxiliary Constructions:
: Points


 * Proof steps:
001. AE = AB [02] & AD = AB [01] ⇒  AD = AE [07]
002. AD = AE [07] ⇒  ∠ADE = ∠DEA [08]
003. AD = AB [01] ⇒  ∠ADB = ∠DBA [09]
004. AE = AB [02] ⇒  ∠AEB = ∠EBA [10]
005. CF = CB [05] & CG = CB [03] ⇒  CG = CF [11]
006. CG = CF [11] ⇒  ∠CGF = ∠GFC [12]
007. ∠CGF = ∠GFC [12] & E,F,D are collinear [06] & G,E,D are collinear [04] ⇒  ∠(CG-DE) = ∠(DE-CF) [13]
008. CG = CB [03] ⇒  ∠CGB = ∠GBC [14]
009. ∠CGB = ∠GBC [14] & A,B,C are collinear [00] ⇒  ∠CGB = ∠GBA [15]
010. CF = CB [05] ⇒  ∠CFB = ∠FBC [16]
011. ∠CFB = ∠FBC [16] & A,B,C are collinear [00] ⇒  ∠CFB = ∠FBA [17]
012. ∠ADE = ∠DEA [08] & ∠ADB = ∠DBA [09] & ∠AEB = ∠EBA [10] & ∠(CG-DE) = ∠(DE-CF) [13] & ∠CGB = ∠GBA [15] & ∠CFB = ∠FBA [17] (Angle chase)⇒  ∠DBG = ∠FBE
==========================

==========================
 * From theorem premises:
B C A D E G : Points
AB ⟂ BC [00]
CD = CB [01]
E,D,G are collinear [02]
A,B,G are collinear [03]
∠BAD = ∠AEG [04]
CB = CE [05]

 * Auxiliary Constructions:
: Points


 * Proof steps:
001. E,D,G are collinear [02] & A,B,G are collinear [03] & ∠BAD = ∠AEG [04] ⇒  ∠AEG = ∠GAD [06]
002. A,B,G are collinear [03] & E,D,G are collinear [02] ⇒  ∠AGE = ∠AGD [07]
003. ∠AEG = ∠GAD [06] & ∠AGE = ∠AGD [07] (Similar Triangles)⇒  EA:AG = AD:DG [08]
004. ∠AEG = ∠GAD [06] & ∠AGE = ∠AGD [07] (Similar Triangles)⇒  EA:EG = AD:AG [09]
005. CD = CB [01] & CB = CE [05] ⇒  C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDE [10]
006. C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDE [10] & AB ⟂ BC [00] ⇒  ∠ABD = ∠BED [11]
007. C is the circumcenter of \Delta BDE [10] & AB ⟂ BC [00] ⇒  ∠EBA = ∠EDB [12]
008. A,B,G are collinear [03] & E,D,G are collinear [02] & ∠ABD = ∠BED [11] ⇒  ∠GBD = ∠BEG [13]
009. E,D,G are collinear [02] & A,B,G are collinear [03] & ∠EDB = ∠EBA [12] ⇒  ∠GDB = ∠EBG [14]
010. ∠GBD = ∠BEG [13] & ∠GDB = ∠EBG [14] (Similar Triangles)⇒  BG:DG = EG:BG [15]
011. EA:AG = AD:DG [08] & EA:EG = AD:AG [09] & BG:DG = EG:BG [15] (Ratio chase)⇒  AG = BG
==========================
